- **ai-duel**: One-shot cross-model comparison: two cold answers, peer critique, then a blind claim-level verdict. Use when the user explicitly requests a second model, second opinion or ai-duel, or when an unresolved major architecture or scientific-method decision warrants independent alternatives. Ordinary comparisons such as "which approach is better" and routine local design choices do not trigger a duel by themselves. Use xreview to revise an existing proposal to consensus; use direct sources for factual or literature lookup. ([minjunnzheng/cross-model-review](https://github.com/minjunnzheng/cross-model-review/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-duel))
